# Current Baton acceptance index

Date: 2026-08-03

Status: hash-bound artifact lookup ledger

This ledger preserves historical and inactive acceptance lookup rows moved
verbatim from the Current Baton table. It has artifact lookup authority only.
Live authority, protected boundaries, active acceptance and next work remain
controlled by `AGENTS.md`; this ledger cannot override them.

| Current product result | The accepted provider-free application session reaches one fixed active-practitioner-directory read in both supervised real Office hosts, and its five authorised lifecycle descendants now pass. Installed Word and Word Online retain independent authored-synthetic practices, principals, sessions, CSRF values and result nonces; each rendered two display-safe rows after a visible action, audited authorization, logged out and rejected reuse. Repeated delivery now revokes surviving authority once and returns an endpoint-free inert page; restored history and repeated action are inert; expired and revoked sessions converge on one sanitized close/reopen state; cookie/surface, CSRF and nonce swaps/replay are denied with zero product reads; a versioned ten-reason ledger contains no identifiers; and the extracted decision adapter remains route-free and absent from `app.main`. Three fresh in-process/PostgreSQL lifecycle scenarios each removed their database, four roles and pools. Continuity 206 / Compass 187 bind `provider_free_office_directory_lifecycle_descendants_pass`; the earlier supervised real-Office evidence remains separately labelled and unchanged. Dependabot alert 17 remains registered `SF-0020`, native-open/`needs_review`. No real identity mapping, live Microsoft/provider interoperability, patient/clinical or other product read, command/write, general endpoint mount, product-table RLS claim, production secret custody, organisational Office deployment, distributed abuse resistance, incident paging/SIEM, deployment, protected integration, production or release is established. |
